# Graph Report - while-we-were-here  (2026-11-22)

## Corpus Check
- 8463 files · ~8,434,403 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 105 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 24, .scss 22, .css 13)

## Summary
- 65205 nodes · 123690 edges · 3264 communities (2502 shown, 762 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 92% EXTRACTED · 8% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 9530 edges (avg confidence: 0.9)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `f7e3fc8b`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
